Western Region oil hub.” }, { id:“TaTU_t03”, uniCode:“TaTU”, uniName:“Takoradi Technical University”, uniType:“Technical”, uniLoc:“Takoradi, Western”, name:“HND Accountancy”, dept:“Business”, cutoff:16, tracks:[“Business”,“General Arts”], requiredElectives:[], requiredCore:[“Maths”,“English”], minGrades:{}, dur:“3 yrs”, fee:“GHS 1,800/yr”, demand:“Moderate”, careers:[“Accounting Technician”,“Finance Clerk”,“Bookkeeper”], note:“Business or Arts students.” }, { id:“TaTU_t04”, uniCode:“TaTU”, uniName:“Takoradi Technical University”, uniType:“Technical”, uniLoc:“Takoradi, Western”, name:“HND Marketing”, dept:“Business”, cutoff:16, tracks:[“Business”,“General Arts”,“General Science”], requiredElectives:[], requiredCore:[“English”], minGrades:{}, dur:“3 yrs”, fee:“GHS 1,800/yr”, demand:“Moderate”, careers:[“Marketing Officer”,“Sales Executive”,“Brand Promoter”], note:“Open to all tracks.” }, // HoTU extras { id:“HoTU_t01”, uniCode:“HoTU”, uniName:“Ho Technical University”, uniType:“Technical”, uniLoc:“Ho, Volta”, name:“HND Mechanical Engineering”, dept:“Engineering”, cutoff:16, tracks:[“General Science”,“Technical”], requiredElectives:[], requiredCore:[“Maths”,“Science”], minGrades:{}, dur:“3 yrs”, fee:“GHS 1,600/yr”, demand:“Low”, careers:[“Mechanical Technician”,“Plant Operator”], note:“Maths and Science required. Volta Region.” }, { id:“HoTU_t02”, uniCode:“HoTU”, uniName:“Ho Technical University”, uniType:“Technical”, uniLoc:“Ho, Volta”, name:“HND Agriculture Technology”, dept:“Agriculture”, cutoff:16, tracks:[“Agricultural Science”,“General Science”], requiredElectives:[“Biology”], requiredCore:[“Science”], minGrades:{}, dur:“3 yrs”, fee:“GHS 1,600/yr”, demand:“Low”, careers:[“Agricultural Technician”,“Farm Manager”], note:“Agricultural background preferred.” }, { id:“HoTU_t03”, uniCode:“HoTU”, uniName:“Ho Technical University”, uniType:“Technical”, uniLoc:“Ho, Volta”, name:“BSc Computer Science”, dept:“Computing”, cutoff:15, tracks:[“General Science”,“General Arts”], requiredElectives:[], requiredCore:[“Maths”], minGrades:{Maths:“C6”}, dur:“4 yrs”, fee:“GHS 1,800/yr”, demand:“Moderate”, careers:[“Software Developer”,“IT Specialist”], note:“Degree-level. Maths required.” }, ]; // ════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ALL_PROGRAMS — Combined from all sources // Total: 1012+ programmes across all Ghanaian accredited institutions // ════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── ALL PROGRAMMES ── const ALL_PROGRAMS = [ // Base explicit programmes from UNIVERSITIES array …UNIVERSITIES.flatMap(u => u.programmes.map((p, idx) => ({ …p, id: u.code + “_” + idx, uniCode: u.code, uniName: u.name, uniType: u.type, uniLoc: u.loc })) ), // Generated programmes (tech unis HND, colleges of education, nursing colleges) …GENERATED_PROGRAMS, // Additional explicit programmes (private unis, health programmes, specialties) …EXTRA_PROGRAMS, // Top-up programmes (specialties, emerging fields) …TOPUP_PROGRAMS, ]; // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ // INTELLIGENT MATCHING ENGINE // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ================================================================ // WASSCE AGGREGATE ENGINE — Official Formula // // KNUST / UG / UCC formula: // Aggregate = English + Core Maths + Integrated Science + 3 relevant electives // (Social Studies is a pass requirement, NOT counted in the aggregate) // // The 3 elective slots are filled as: // Step 1: Lock required electives for this programme (e.g. Biology for Medicine) // Step 2: Fill remaining slots from student’s other electives (best grades first) // Step 3: Sum = English + Maths + Science + those 3 electives // // Example (Medicine): Required = Biology, Chemistry, Physics // English A1(1) + Maths A1(1) + Science A1(1) + Biology B2(2) + Chem A1(1) + Phys A1(1) = 7 // // Example (Engineering): Required = Elective Maths, Physics // English A1(1) + Maths A1(1) + Science A1(1) + El.Maths C6(6) + Phys A1(1) + [best other](1) = 11 // (Note: Biology is NOT counted here even if student has A1 — not relevant to Engineering) // ================================================================ // Global best-6 aggregate (for display / quick calculator only) // Programme-specific aggregate — the accurate calculation used for matching // Formula: English + Core Maths + Integrated Science + 3 relevant electives // ── AGGREGATE ENGINE ── // ── AGGREGATE ENGINE ── const calcProgAggregate = (prog, coreGrades, electivesWithGrades) => { // ================================================================ // OFFICIAL WASSCE AGGREGATE FORMULA (programme-aware) // // Standard formula: English + Core Maths + BEST(Science OR Social) + 3 relevant electives // // The 3rd core slot (Science vs Social Studies) depends on: // 1. KNUST: uses Science for ALL programmes EXCEPT Law & Political Science // (where Social Studies is used instead) // 2. UMaT: always uses Science over Social Studies // 3. All other universities: pick the BETTER of Science or Social Studies // BUT science-based programmes prefer Science, arts/social prefer Social // // Nursing at KNUST: General Science track only // The 3 elective slots: required electives first, then best remaining // ================================================================ const engPt = GP_MAP[coreGrades.english] || null; const mthPt = GP_MAP[coreGrades.maths] || null; const sciPt = GP_MAP[coreGrades.science] || null; const socPt = GP_MAP[coreGrades.social] || null; if (!engPt || !mthPt) return null; // English and Maths always required // ── Determine which 3rd core subject to use ── const uniCode = (prog.uniCode || “”).replace(/_.*/, “”); // strip suffixes const progName = (prog.name || “”).toLowerCase(); const isKNUST = uniCode === “KNUST” || uniCode === “KNUST2”; const isUMaT = uniCode === “UMaT”; // KNUST Law & Political Science use Social Studies instead of Science const knustSocialProg = isKNUST && ( progName.includes(“law”) || progName.includes(“political science”) || progName.includes(“communication & media”) || progName.includes(“geography”) || progName.includes(“history”) || progName.includes(“sociology”) || progName.includes(“french”) || progName.includes(“english language”) ); let thirdCorePt; if (isKNUST && !knustSocialProg) { // KNUST: always uses Science (not Social) for science/engineering/business/health thirdCorePt = sciPt; } else if (isKNUST && knustSocialProg) { // KNUST Law, Political Science etc: uses Social Studies thirdCorePt = socPt; } else if (isUMaT) { // UMaT: always Science over Social thirdCorePt = sciPt; } else { // All other universities: pick the BETTER (lower number = better) of Science/Social // But also consider what the programme requires: // Science-based tracks prefer Science; Arts/Social/Business prefer Social const sciTrack = prog.tracks && ( prog.tracks.includes(“General Science”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Technical”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Agricultural Science”) ); const artsTrack = prog.tracks && ( prog.tracks.includes(“General Arts”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Business”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Home Economics”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Visual Arts”) ); ``` if (sciTrack && !artsTrack) { // Science programme — use Science if available, fall back to Social thirdCorePt = sciPt || socPt; } else if (artsTrack && !sciTrack) { // Arts/Social programme — use Social if available, fall back to Science thirdCorePt = socPt || sciPt; } else { // Mixed or open tracks — pick whichever is better (lower = better grade) if (sciPt && socPt) { thirdCorePt = Math.min(sciPt, socPt); } else { thirdCorePt = sciPt || socPt; } } ``` } if (!thirdCorePt) return null; // need at least 3 cores const corePts = [engPt, mthPt, thirdCorePt]; // exactly 3 cores // ── Required electives for this programme ── const required = prog.requiredElectives || []; // Step 1: Lock required electives the student has const requiredPts = []; for (const req of required) { const found = electivesWithGrades.find(e => e.subject === req && e.grade); if (found) requiredPts.push(GP_MAP[found.grade] || 9); // If missing required elective, subject check will flag ineligible } // Step 2: Fill remaining slots from student’s OTHER electives (best grades first) const slotsNeeded = 3 - requiredPts.length; const optionalPts = electivesWithGrades .filter(e => !required.includes(e.subject) && e.grade && (GP_MAP[e.grade] || 9) <= 8) .map(e => GP_MAP[e.grade]) .sort((a, b) => a - b) .slice(0, Math.max(0, slotsNeeded)); // Step 3: Total = 3 cores + up to 3 electives const all = […corePts, …requiredPts, …optionalPts]; if (all.length < 4) return null; return all.reduce((s, p) => s + p, 0); }; // ── SUBJECT REQUIREMENTS CHECKER ── // ── SUBJECT CHECKER ── const checkSubjectRequirements = (prog, studentTrack, studentElectives, studentCoreGrades, electivesWithGrades) => { const issues = []; const passes = []; const uniCode = (prog.uniCode || “”).replace(/_.*/, “”); const progName = (prog.name || “”).toLowerCase(); // ── 1. TRACK CHECK ── if (prog.tracks && prog.tracks.length > 0) { if (!prog.tracks.includes(studentTrack)) { issues.push(`Requires ${prog.tracks.join(" or ")} track (you are on ${studentTrack} track)`); } else { passes.push(`✓ Your ${studentTrack} track qualifies`); } } // ── 2. SPECIAL KNUST NURSING / MIDWIFERY RULE ── // KNUST Nursing and Midwifery are General Science ONLY — no exceptions const isKNUST = uniCode === “KNUST” || uniCode === “KNUST2”; const isNursingOrMidwifery = progName.includes(“nursing”) || progName.includes(“midwifery”); if (isKNUST && isNursingOrMidwifery && studentTrack !== “General Science”) { issues.push(“KNUST Nursing and Midwifery are strictly for General Science students only”); } // ── 3. REQUIRED ELECTIVES CHECK ── if (prog.requiredElectives && prog.requiredElectives.length > 0) { prog.requiredElectives.forEach(req => { if (!studentElectives.includes(req)) { issues.push(`Requires ${req} as an elective (not in your combination)`); } else { passes.push(`✓ You have ${req}`); } }); } else { passes.push(“✓ No specific elective combination required”); } // ── 4. REQUIRED CORE SUBJECTS CHECK ── if (prog.requiredCore && prog.requiredCore.length > 0) { prog.requiredCore.forEach(req => { const coreMap = { “English”:“english”, “Maths”:“maths”, “Science”:“science”, “Social Studies”:“social” }; const key = coreMap[req]; if (key && !studentCoreGrades[key]) { issues.push(`Core ${req} grade is required`); } else if (key) { passes.push(`✓ Core ${req} present`); } }); } // ── 5. SCIENCE CORE REQUIREMENT ── // KNUST (non-social programmes) and UMaT require Integrated Science // If student has no science grade for these universities, flag it const isUMaT = uniCode === “UMaT”; const knustSocialProg = isKNUST && ( progName.includes(“law”) || progName.includes(“political science”) || progName.includes(“geography”) || progName.includes(“history”) || progName.includes(“sociology”) || progName.includes(“french”) || progName.includes(“english language”) || progName.includes(“communication & media”) ); if ((isKNUST && !knustSocialProg) || isUMaT) { if (!studentCoreGrades.science) { issues.push(`${uniCode} requires Integrated Science grade for this programme`); } else { passes.push(`✓ Integrated Science grade present (required by ${uniCode})`); } } // ── 6. MINIMUM GRADE CHECKS ── if (prog.minGrades && electivesWithGrades) { Object.entries(prog.minGrades).forEach(([subj, minGrade]) => { const coreMap = { “English”:“english”, “Maths”:“maths”, “Science”:“science”, “Social Studies”:“social” }; const coreKey = coreMap[subj]; let studentGrade = null; ``` if (coreKey) { studentGrade = studentCoreGrades[coreKey]; } else { const found = (electivesWithGrades || []).find(e => e.subject === subj); if (found) studentGrade = found.grade; } if (studentGrade) { const minPts = GP_MAP[minGrade] || 9; const stuPts = GP_MAP[studentGrade] || 9; if (stuPts > minPts) { issues.push(`${subj} requires minimum ${minGrade} (you have ${studentGrade})`); } else { passes.push(`✓ ${subj} grade ${studentGrade} meets minimum ${minGrade}`); } } }); ``` } return { eligible: issues.length === 0, issues, passes }; }; // ================================================================ // SHARED COMPONENTS // ================================================================ // ── LOGO ── // ── NEWS TICKER ITEMS ── // ── ANNOUNCEMENTS TICKER ── const ANNOUNCEMENTS = [ “🎓 2025/2026 Admissions Now Open — UG, KNUST, UCC, GIMPA”, “📢 WASSCE Results Released — Check Your Eligibility Now”, “🔥 New Cut-Off Points Updated for 2025/2026 Academic Year”, “⭐ Premium Plan: Check All 42 Universities for Just ₵15”, ]; // ── NAV ── // ── STICKY BOTTOM BAR ── // ── TOASTS ── // ── AUTH MODAL ── // ================================================================ // HOME PAGE // ================================================================ // ================================================================ // CHECKER PAGE — exact match to screenshots 1-11 // ================================================================ const CheckerPage = ({ go, user, addToast, auth }) => { const STEPS = [“SHS Track”,“Your Profile”,“Core Subjects”,“Elective Subjects”,“Review & Pay”,“Results”]; const [step, setStep] = useState(1); const [f, setF] = useState({ track:””, examType:“WASSCE”, gender:””, phone:””, careers:””, preferredProg:””, preferredUni:””, coreGrades:{ english:””, maths:””, science:””, social:”” }, electives:[], elGrades:{}, }); const [results, setResults] = useState(null); const [resTab, setResTab] = useState(“qualified”); const [showPayGate, setShowPG]= useState(false); const [payStep, setPayStep] = useState(“plan”); const [selPlan, setSelPlan] = useState(“premium”); const [payMethod, setPayMethod] = useState(“mtn”); const [payPhone, setPayPhone] = useState(””); const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false); const up = (k,v) => setF(p => ({…p, [k]:v})); const upCore = (k,v) => setF(p => ({…p, coreGrades:{…p.coreGrades,[k]:v}})); const trackElectives = f.track && TRACKS[f.track] ? TRACKS[f.track].electives : []; const toggleEl = subj => { setF(p => { const has = p.electives.includes(subj); const newEl = has ? p.electives.filter(e=>e!==subj) : […p.electives, subj]; const newGr = {…p.elGrades}; if (has) delete newGr[subj]; return {…p, electives:newEl, elGrades:newGr}; }); }; const runAnalysis = () => { const cg = f.coreGrades; const ewg = f.electives.map(s => ({subject:s, grade:f.elGrades[s]||””})); const globalAgg = calcAggregate({ english:cg.english, maths:cg.maths, …Object.fromEntries(ewg.filter(e=>e.grade).map(e=>[e.subject,e.grade])) }); ``` const programsToCheck = selPlan==="basic" ? ALL_PROGRAMS.filter(p => p.uniCode===f.preferredUni || p.uniCode?.startsWith(f.preferredUni)) : ALL_PROGRAMS; const qualified=[], near=[], blocked=[]; programsToCheck.forEach(prog => { const chk = checkSubjectRequirements(prog, f.track, f.electives, cg, ewg); const agg = calcProgAggregate(prog, cg, ewg); const cutoff = prog.cutoff || 30; const matchPct = agg ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((1 - (agg-6)/(cutoff-4)) * 100))) : 0; if (!chk.eligible) { blocked.push({...prog, issues:chk.issues, passes:chk.passes, agg, matchPct}); } else if (agg && agg <= cutoff) { qualified.push({...prog, issues:[], passes:chk.passes, agg, matchPct, cutoff}); } else { near.push({...prog, issues:chk.issues.length?chk.issues:[`Aggregate ${agg} exceeds cut-off ${cutoff}`], passes:chk.passes, agg, matchPct, cutoff}); } }); qualified.sort((a,b) => (b.matchPct||0)-(a.matchPct||0)); near.sort((a,b)=>(a.agg||99)-(b.agg||99)); // Scholarships — static list const schols = [ {name:"Ghana Scholarship Secretariat",org:"Government of Ghana",amount:"Full tuition",deadline:"2025-08-30"}, {name:"GETFUND Scholarship",org:"Ghana Education Trust Fund",amount:"Tuition fees",deadline:"2025-09-15"}, {name:"Mastercard Foundation",org:"Mastercard Foundation",amount:"Full + living",deadline:"2025-10-01"}, {name:"MTN Ghana Foundation",org:"MTN Ghana",amount:"GHS 5,000/yr",deadline:"2025-07-31"}, {name:"TotalEnergies STEM",org:"TotalEnergies Ghana",amount:"GHS 6,000/yr",deadline:"2025-08-15"}, ].filter(()=>true);
